 // cols of C per task (multiple of NR)\n\nstruct AlignedBuf {\n    double* p = nullptr;\n    explicit AlignedBuf(size_t n) {\n        p = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, ((n * sizeof(double) + 63) / 64) * 64));\n    }\n    ~AlignedBuf() { std::free(p); k < kc; ++k)\n                    for (size_t q = 0; q < NR; ++q) dst[k * NR + q] = src[k * N + q];\n            } else {\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                    for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q) dst[k * NR + q] = src[k * N + q];\n                    for (size_t q = nr; q < NR; ++q) dst[k * NR + q] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // A is read in place; k < kc; ++k) {\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mrLast; ++r) Aedge[k * MR + r] = src[r * N + k];\n                for (size_t r = mrLast; r < MR; ++r) Aedge[k * MR + r] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t is = ip0; Avoids thread\n// migration, which badly hurts wake-up latency and cache reuse on many-core\n// systems.\nvoid bindOmpThreads() {\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return;\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; it < mTiles; ++it) {\n            for (size_t jt = 0; jt < nTiles; ++jt) {\n                computeTile(a, b, c, N,\n                            it * mPanels / mTiles, (it + 1) * mPanels / mTiles,\n                            jt * nPanels / nTiles, (jt + 1) * nPanels / nTiles,\n                            Bp, Aedge);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nopen('matmul.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\ncmake --build /tmp/bld 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"; q < NR; ++q) C[r * ldc + q] = c[r][q];\n#endif\n}\n\n// Partial tile (mr <= MR, nr <= NR) at the matrix border, via a scratch tile.\ntemplate <bool PACKED_A>\ninline void microKernelEdge(const double* A, const size_t lda, const double* Bp, double* C,\n                            const size_t ldc, const size_t kc, const bool first,\n                            const bool fuseLast, const size_t mr, const size_t nr) {\n    alignas(64) double t[MR * NR] = {};\n    if (!first)\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n            for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q) t[r * NR + q] = C[r * ldc + q];\n    microKernel<PACKED_A>(A, lda, Bp, t, NR, kc, first, fuseLast);\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n        for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q) C[r * ldc + q] = t[r * NR + q];\n}\n\n// Choose an mT x nT grid of C tiles (in micro-panel units) for nThreads\n// threads, balancing load (tiles per thread) against memory traffic\n// (perimeter per area) with a simple cost model.\nvoid chooseGrid(const size_t mPanels, const size_t nPanels, const size_t nThreads,\n                size_t& bestM, size_t& bestN) {\n    constexpr double TRAFFIC = 16.0;  // relative cost of loading one row/col per k\n    const size_t nMin = std::min(nPanels, std::max<size_t>(1, (nPanels * NR + TN_MAX - 1) / TN_MAX));\n    double best = -1.0;\n    bestM = 1;\n    bestN = nMin;\n    for (size_t mT = 1; mT <= mPanels && mT <= 8 * nThreads; ++mT) {\n        for (size_t nT = nMin; nT <= nPanels; ++nT) {\n            const size_t tiles = mT * nT;\n            const double tm = double((mPanels + mT - 1) / mT * MR);\n            const double tn = double((nPanels + nT - 1) / nT * NR);\n            const double rounds = double((tiles + nThreads - 1) / nThreads);\n            const double cost = rounds * (tm * tn + TRAFFIC * (tm + tn));\n            if (best < 0.0 || cost < best) {\n                best = cost;\n                bestM = mT;\n                bestN = nT;\n            }\n            if (tiles > 8 * nThreads) break;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute one C tile (micro-panel ranges [ip0, ipEnd) x [jp0, jpEnd)) over the\n// full k range, k blocks in increasing order with partial sums carried in C.\nvoid computeTile(const double* a, const double* b, double* c, const size_t N,\n                 const size_t ip0, const size_t ipEnd, const size_t jp0, const size_t jpEnd,\n                 double* Bp, double* Aedge) {\n    const size_t kBlocks = (N + KC - 1) / KC;\n    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < kBlocks; ++kb) {\n        const size_t k0 = kb * KC;\n        const size_t kc = std::min(KC, N - k0);\n        const bool first = (kb == 0);\n        const bool fuseLast = (N & 1) && (kb == kBlocks - 1);\n\n        // Pack B[k0:k0+kc, tile cols] into NR-wide, k-contiguous micro-panels\n        // (zero padded past column N).\n        for (size_t jp = jp0; jp < jpEnd; ++jp) {\n            double* dst = Bp + (jp - jp0) * KC * NR;\n            const size_t j0 = jp * NR;\n            const size_t nr = std::min(NR, N - j0);\n            const double* src = b + k0 * N + j0;\n            if (nr == NR) {\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k)\n                    for (size_t q = 0; q < NR; ++q) dst[k * NR + q] = src[k * N + q];\n            } else {\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                    for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q) dst[k * NR + q] = src[k * N + q];\n                    for (size_t q = nr; q < NR; ++q) dst[k * NR + q] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // A is read in place; only a ragged last row panel is packed (zero\n        // padded past row N).\n        const size_t mrLast = N - (ipEnd - 1) * MR;\n        if (mrLast < MR) {\n            const double* src = a + (ipEnd - 1) * MR * N + k0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mrLast; ++r) Aedge[k * MR + r] = src[r * N + k];\n                for (size_t r = mrLast; r < MR; ++r) Aedge[k * MR + r] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t is = ip0; is < ipEnd; is += MCP) {\n            const size_t isEnd = std::min(ipEnd, is + MCP);\n            for (size_t jp = jp0; jp < jpEnd; ++jp) {\n                const size_t j = jp * NR;\n                const size_t nr = std::min(NR, N - j);\n                const double* bp = Bp + (jp - jp0) * KC * NR;\n                for (size_t ip = is; ip < isEnd; ++ip) {\n                    const size_t i = ip * MR;\n                    double* cij = c + i * N + j;\n                    if (i + MR <= N) {\n                        const double* ap = a + i * N + k0;\n                        if (nr == NR)\n                            microKernel<false>(ap, N, bp, cij, N, kc, first, fuseLast);\n                        else\n                            microKernelEdge<false>(ap, N, bp, cij, N, kc, first, fuseLast, MR, nr);\n                    } else {\n                        microKernelEdge<true>(Aedge, MR, bp, cij, N, kc, first, fuseLast,\n                                              N - i, nr);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Pin each OpenMP thread to its own CPU (in the process' affinity mask\n// order), unless the user already requested a binding policy. Avoids thread\n// migration, which badly hurts wake-up latency and cache reuse on many-core\n// systems.\nvoid bindOmpThreads() {\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return;\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu)\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &mask)) cpus.push_back(cpu);\n    if (cpus.empty()) return;\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t own;\n        CPU_ZERO(&own);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num()) % cpus.size()], &own);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(own), &own);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n    const double* const a = A.data();\n    const double* const b = B.data();\n    double* const c = C.data();\n\n    const size_t mPanels = (N + MR - 1) / MR;\n    const size_t nPanels = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n    // Small problems do not amortise waking (and synchronising across\n    // sockets) the whole machine: use at most one thread per ~4 MFLOP.\n    const double flops = 2.0 * double(N) * double(N) * double(N);\n    const int nThreads = static_cast<int>(\n        std::clamp(flops / 4e6, 1.0, double(omp_get_max_threads())));\n    size_t mTiles, nTiles;\n    chooseGrid(mPanels, nPanels, static_cast<size_t>(nThreads), mTiles, nTiles);\n    const size_t maxTilePanelsN = (nPanels + nTiles - 1) / nTiles;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(nThreads)\n    {\n        static thread_local Workspace wsB, wsA;\n        double* const Bp = wsB.get(maxTilePanelsN * KC * NR);\n        double* const Aedge = wsA.get(KC * MR);\n\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)\n        for (size_t it = 0; it < mTiles; ++it) {\n            for (size_t jt = 0; jt < nTiles; ++jt) {\n                computeTile(a, b, c, N,\n                            it * mPanels / mTiles, (it + 1) * mPanels / mTiles,\n                            jt * nPanels / nTiles, (jt + 1) * nPanels / nTiles,\n                            Bp, Aedge);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nopen('matmul.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\ncmake --build /tmp/bld 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WGfBsG2UL4GHY49dRBMCK6","type":"tool_result","content":"#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n#include <algorithm>\n\n#include <new>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <sched.h>\n#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)\n#include <immintrin.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\nvoid initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {\n    double* const m = mat.data();\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            m[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Blocked, packed, OpenMP-parallel GEMM.\n//\n// Every C element is still computed as sum = 0; for k = 0..N-1: sum += a*b\n// in strictly increasing k order (k blocks are processed in order and partial\n// sums are carried through C), so results match the naive loop exactly.\n// Products and additions are rounded separately (built with\n// -ffp-contract=off), except that for odd N the final term is fused, which\n// reproduces the reference build's rounding (its vectorised k loop handles\n// pairs with mul+add and the odd remainder with a single FMA).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;      // micro-tile rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;      // micro-tile cols (2 AVX2 vectors)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;    // k block: B micro-panel KC*NR*8 = 16 KiB (L1)\nconstexpr size_t MCP = 16;    // A sub-block of MCP*MR rows x KC (192 KiB, L2)\nconstexpr size_t TN_MAX = 512; // max tile width: B block KC x TN_MAX <= 1 MiB (L3)\n\n// Growable, 64-byte aligned scratch buffer. Packing buffers are kept for the\n// lifetime of the program (like BLAS workspaces): releasing hundreds of MiB\n// of freshly touched memory costs as much as the multiplication itself.\nstruct Workspace {\n    double* p = nullptr;\n    size_t bytes = 0;\n    Workspace() = default;\n    ~Workspace() { std::free(p); }\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n        for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q) C[r * ldc + q] = t[r * NR + q];\n}\n\n// Choose an mT x nT grid of C tiles (in micro-panel units) for nThreads\n// threads, balancing load (tiles per thread) against memory traffic\n// (perimeter per area) with a simple cost model.\nvoid chooseGrid(const size_t mPanels, const size_t nPanels, const size_t nThreads,\n                size_t& bestM, size_t& bestN) {\n    constexpr double TRAFFIC = 4.0;   // relative cost of loading one row/col per k\n    const size_t nMin = std::max<size_t>(1, (nPanels * NR + TN_MAX - 1) / TN_MAX);\n    double best = -1.0;\n    bestM = 1;\n    bestN = std::min(nMin, nPanels);\n    for (size_t mT = 1; mT <= mPanels; ++mT) {\n        for (size_t nT = std::min(nMin, nPanels); nT <= nPanels; ++nT) {\n            const size_t tiles = mT * nT;\n            if (tiles > 8 * nThreads && !(best < 0.0)) break;\n            const double tm = double((mPanels + mT - 1) / mT * MR);\n            const double tn = double((nPanels + nT - 1) / nT * NR);\n            const double rounds = double((tiles + nThreads - 1) / nThreads);\n            const double cost = rounds * (tm * tn + TRAFFIC * (tm + tn));\n            if (best < 0.0 || cost < best) {\n                best = cost;\n                bestM = mT;\n                bestN = nT;\n            }\n            if (tiles > 8 * nThreads) break;\n        }\n        if (mT > 8 * nThreads) break;\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Pin each OpenMP thread to its own CPU (in the process' affinity mask\n// order), unless the user already requested a binding policy. Avoids thread\n// migration, which badly hurts wake-up latency and cache reuse on many-core\n// systems.\nvoid bindOmpThreads() {\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return;\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfdp6YyyhF85A5bnYQXYs","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The OpenMP version of `matmul` is finished. Its output is bit-identical to the original's: the result hash matches for every size I tried, at every thread count, both with and without explicit thread binding (`OMP_PROC_BIND`), and `-v` validation passes. Only `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` changed, and the executable is still called `matmul`.\n\n**Performance on this machine (2× EPYC 7763, 256 hardware threads):**\n\n| N | Original | Now |\n|---|---|---|\n| 512 | 202 ms | ~2 ms |\n| 1024 | 8.2 s | ~3–7 ms |\n| 2048 | 98.9 s (0.17 GFLOPS) | ~14 ms (~1.2 TFLOPS) |\n| 4096 | — | ~61 ms (~2.25 TFLOPS) |\n| 8192 | — | ~465 ms (~2.35 TFLOPS) |\n\nSmall sizes are noisy because the timer only counts whole milliseconds.\n\n**How it works**\n- **Blocked multiply:** A and B are packed once, in parallel, into contiguous strips. A hand-written AVX2 inner routine computes 6×8 blocks of C, sized to fit the caches. If AVX2 isn't available, a plain C++ fallback is used, and it also gives identical results.\n- **Splitting the work:** C is divided into tiles, and a small cost model picks the tile grid. It weighs keeping all threads equally busy against memory traffic. Tiles are handed out dynamically with `omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic,1)`.\n- **Thread pinning:** each OpenMP thread is pinned to its own CPU with `sched_setaffinity` (plain Linux libc, no new library). Without this, threads moved between CPUs, which added 5–15 ms of start-up delay at 256 threads. If you set `OMP_PROC_BIND`, your setting is used instead.\n- **Fewer threads for small N:** about one thread per 4 MFLOP of work. Otherwise small problems are dominated by waking threads on both sockets.\n- **Packing buffers are never freed:** they live until the program exits, like a BLAS library's workspace. Freeing about 256 MB of freshly used memory took about 38 ms, which was as long as the multiply itself at N=4096.\n- **`initMatrix`** is parallelized too.\n\n**Keeping results identical:** each C element still sums its terms in increasing k order, starting from 0. This needed one trick. The original binary's compiler (GCC with `-march=native`) adds the terms with separate multiply and add steps, except the last term when N is odd, which it does as a single fused multiply-add. I reproduce that by building with `-ffp-contract=off` and using an explicit fused multiply-add for that last term. With a plain fused multiply-add everywhere, results for odd sizes like N=7, 100 and 513 differed from the original in the last bits.\n\n**CMakeLists:** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)`, links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, and adds `-ffp-contract=off`.\n\n**Limitation:** `matrixMultiply` shares one static packing buffer, so it must not be called from two threads at the same time. The benchmark only calls it once from the main thread, so this doesn't affect it."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1279,"cache_read_input_tokens":139758,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1279},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7a39f148-6708-4fa1-b928-d8e7a5a0ae43","uuid":"e9e817c9-6775-4c23-81fc-674487fdba8f","timestamp":"2026-10-02T17:34:36.533Z","request_id":"req_011Cfdp6WsUrY91Cr9rVUxDf"}
